Method: 提出分辨率感知的物理支撑推断框架：1) 建立跨字典置信对应，保留校准兼容字典和部署兼容稀疏表示，投影到物理支撑空间；2) 推导局部相干原子类（分离尺度 s）的最小极大物理分辨率理论界限 δ_opt(N,s) ≍ min{s, 1/(√N s²)}，相对分辨率由方向信息尺度 N s⁶ 决定；3) 分析部署复制改善物理定位的条件；4) 开发主动端点括号法（AEB），一种自适应有限候选集过程，仅评估仍能影响物理报告的候选，否则安全粗化或弃权。

Result: 理论上确立了最小极大物理分辨率界限及方向信息尺度的作用；揭示了部署复制仅在方向变化无法通过调整活性系数吸收时才能改善物理定位；实验表明点值插入式选择器可能在物理上过度精确，而 AEB 能避免无支持的细化，且候选评估次数更少。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种严谨的物理支撑推断方法论，解决了高相干字典下稀疏追踪物理解释不可靠的问题，理论界限揭示了分辨率的基本权衡，AEB 算法在计算效率和物理可靠性之间取得良好平衡，为实际部署中的物理定位提供了可信框架。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [174] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BERT-LER，一种基于 BERT 的结构化电子健康记录（EHR）预测模型，通过百分位分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散 token 并保留分级信息，结合 Integrated Gradients 实现 token 级可解释性。在 EHRShot 基准和哮喘严重程度进展真实世界研究中，模型预测性能具有竞争力，且在实验室相关任务上常优于现有基准，解释结果与临床已知风险因素一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化 EHR 预测模型鲜有同时重视定量实验室信息与输入医疗事件的可解释性，存在方法学空白。

Method: 基于 7500 万去标识化患者数据预训练并微调 BERT 风格模型；将实验室检验结果通过百分位分箱编码为离散 token，保留分级信息；采用 Integrated Gradients 对输入 EHR 序列中的 token 进行归因分析，提供可解释性。

Result: 在公共 EHRShot 基准套件和哮喘严重程度进展真实世界任务中，BERT-LER 预测性能与基准模型相当，在实验室相关任务上常超越基准；token 级归因与临床已知风险因素一致，说明可解释性良好。

Conclusion: BERT-LER 统一了实验室数值表示与可解释性，填补了 EHR 基础模型的方法学空白，且架构和解释方法可推广至多治疗领域和预测任务。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [175] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在相同部署条件下（顶装安装、同步数据、统一CNN模型）对比了FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi感知在医疗监测场景下的性能，实验涉及20名受试者、6种房间布局，覆盖精细10类活动识别和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务。结果显示：IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别上表现最佳（宏F1 89.0%），FMCW对未见房间布局泛化能力最强（宏F1 83.8%），睡眠监测三种技术均超92%宏F1。研究揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性的本质权衡，并归因于距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RF非接触医疗监测研究多基于不同硬件、数据集和评估方法，缺乏公平对比；且顶装雷达部署在医疗环境中具有实用性和成本优势，但其性能表现尚未充分探索。因此需要在受控条件下系统对比主流射频技术，为医疗RF感知系统设计提供实证指导。

Method: 采用同步记录方式，从20名参与者在6种房间布局中收集FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三模态数据。统一使用相同的卷积神经网络（CNN）模型，分别在细粒度10类人体活动识别（HAR）任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务上评估三种技术。实验设计包含跨受试者和跨房间布局的泛化测试。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别任务上达到最高宏F1 89.0%；FMCW在跨房间布局泛化任务上表现最优，宏F1 83.8%。睡眠监测任务中，三种技术在未见环境下均超过92%宏F1。分析表明：IR-UWB凭借高距离分辨率和天线分集擅长精细动作识别；FMCW凭借优异多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留具有更强环境鲁棒性；Wi-Fi感知受限于较低分辨率但部署成本最低。

Conclusion: 研究证实了射频感知中识别精度与环境鲁棒性存在根本权衡，这种权衡可由底层物理层特性（距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率、空间信息保留）解释。为医疗RF感知系统选型提供实用准则：精细活动识别优先选IR-UWB，复杂多变环境长期监测优先选FMCW，成本敏感场景可考虑Wi-Fi感知。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


### [176] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LSTM循环神经网络的监督学习模型，用于估算直升机起飞重量，利用空客全球机队数据，遵循EASA和Eurocae ED-324的学习保障流程，验证了模型在遗留航空电子计算机上的部署可行性，适用于机载关键功能如机上报警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决直升机起飞重量估算问题，利用机器学习提高估算精度，同时满足航空认证要求（EASA、Eurocae ED-324），实现关键航空功能的机载部署。

Method: 采用监督学习，基于长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络，使用空客全球在役机队大量数据集训练；制定机器学习需求、模型描述及实现，遵循学习保障流程，并在遗留航空电子计算机上验证部署。

Result: 成功实现了符合认证要求的LSTM重量估算模型，验证了需求，证明了在遗留航空电子硬件上部署的可行性，适用于机载报警等关键功能。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了机器学习模型在航空关键功能中的可行部署路径，通过学习保障流程满足认证标准，为直升机起飞重量估算提供了可靠的机载解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Quantization Beyond Uniform Bit Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19388)
*K. S. Sreeramji,Sabyasachi Basu,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Kirankumar Shiragur,Yujia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变长位分配框架，针对具有Matryoshka性质的嵌入向量，通过非均匀位分配在相同存储预算下显著提升PQ和SQ的量化质量，尤其是在低比特率下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方案均匀分配比特，忽略了嵌入向量的几何结构（如Matryoshka性质），导致低比特率下效率低下。

Method: 提出变长位分配框架，将嵌入划分为连续桶，非均匀分配存储；采用贪心分配策略，应用于乘积量化(PQ)和标量量化(SQ)。

Result: 在具有Matryoshka性质的嵌入上实验，非均匀分配优于均匀基线；相同压缩率下，PQ召回率提升高达8%，SQ提升高达18%，低比特率下改进最大。

Conclusion: 变长位分配为大规模检索系统提供了结构感知压缩和索引的新方向。 }

Abstract: Quantization is a fundamental technique to handle the growing sizes of embeddings generated by modern models. Existing quantization schemes are largely embedding agnostic and allocate bits uniformly across dimensions. However, recent models produce embeddings with significant geometric structure. In this work, we investigate whether a variable bit allocation scheme can improve quantization quality under a fixed memory budget. We propose a simple variable bit allocation framework that partitions an embedding into contiguous buckets and allocates storage non-uniformly across them. Using a greedy allocation strategy, we instantiate this framework for both Product Quantization (PQ) and Scalar Quantization (SQ). We perform a series of experiments on embeddings known to have the Matryoshka property (MRL), and consistently observe that non-uniform allocations outperform uniform baselines at identical storage budgets. The largest improvements occur in the low-bit regime, where uniform allocation is particularly inefficient for MRL embeddings. At the same compression rates, variable allocation improves recall by up to 8\% for PQ and up to 18\% for SQ. Our results suggest a new direction for structure-aware compression and indexing techniques for large-scale retrieval systems.

</details>


### [178] [HARP: Hierarchical Adaptive Ranking with Preference-Adaptive Fusion for Query-Based CVE Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19430)
*Haochen Liu,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Yanchi Liu,Jundong Li,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出HARP框架，解决漏洞优先级排序中隐性偏好难以显式表达的问题，利用历史标注案例和漏洞知识图谱进行多视图融合排序，实验证明其优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞优先级排序本质上依赖于组织的操作偏好，同一CVE在不同偏好场景下优先级不同。现有评分系统假设固定标准，而实际组织偏好往往是隐性的、难以用文本提示表达的，但历史验证过的分拣案例更容易获取。因此需要一种能利用历史标注案例而非显式偏好描述的查询式CVE优先级排序方法。

Method: HARP是一个基于图的多视图框架：1）从漏洞知识图谱检索证据；2）用策略条件下的全局视图、企业视图和用户视图对候选漏洞评分；3）从采样的历史标注案例中拟合视图融合权重；最后结合自然语言查询和历史案例库对候选漏洞进行排序。

Result: 在三种偏好场景和多种主干LLM上的实验显示，HARP优于多个基线方法，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HARP通过结合漏洞知识图谱证据检索、多视图策略条件评分和历史案例驱动的视图融合，成功解决了隐性偏好场景下的CVE优先级排序问题，无需显式文本偏好描述即可实现优于现有方法的排序性能。

Abstract: Vulnerability prioritization is inherently preference dependent, since the same CVE can receive different remediation priority under different operational preference scenarios. Existing scoring systems and ranking methods typically assume a fixed criterion. In practice, organizations already operate under a preference scenario, but this preference is often implicit and difficult to express as a written prompt instruction, while triage queries usually do not encode it. Past validated triage cases under the current scenario are more readily available. We study query-based CVE prioritization in this setting and propose HARP, a graph-grounded multi-view framework that ranks candidates from a natural-language query together with a support bank of historical labeled examples from the current preference scenario, without requiring an explicit textual summary of that scenario. HARP retrieves evidence from a vulnerability knowledge graph, scores candidates with policy-conditioned global, enterprise, and user views, and fits view-fusion weights from sampled supports. Experiments across three preference scenarios and multiple backbone LLMs show that HARP outperforms multiple baselines, expressing our method's effectiveness.

</details>


### [179] [SSR-GRPO: Integrating Supervision and Semantic IDs into Reinforcement Learning for Dense Retrieval in E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19595)
*Guangxin Song,Xing Fang,Mingmin Jin,Jing Wang,Bokang Wang,Zhentao Song,Junjie Bai,Jianbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSR-GRPO，通过语义标识符（SIDs）和密集向量的双视角相关性评估，结合硬负样本挖掘与掩码机制，解决了 R-GRPO 中的噪声候选和偏置奖励问题，在电商搜索中显著提升检索效果并已大规模部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的检索（EBR）在处理复杂隐性语义时表现不足；R-GRPO 虽然引入强化学习，但受限于批次采样导致的噪声 top-K 候选，以及使用同类训练的 LLM 作为奖励模型带来的偏置相关性评估，限制了其效果。

Method: 提出 SSR-GRPO：1) 双视角相关性评估框架，融合量化学习产生的语义标识符（SIDs）与密集向量，生成更无偏的相关性分数；2) 利用 SIDs 的层级相似关系挖掘硬负样本，设计掩码函数过滤组内噪声样本，并构建 Retrieval-DPO 任务，从成对视角学习细粒度语义区分。

Result: 大量离线和在线实验验证了 SSR-GRPO 的有效性，已在大规模电商平台部署并取得显著提升。

Conclusion: SSR-GRPO 通过语义标识符增强的相关性建模与硬负样本驱动的双重优化，有效解决了密集检索中的噪声与偏置问题，为电商搜索提供了可落地的强化学习范式。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval (EBR) is pivotal in e-commerce search but often struggles with complex semantics. While recent methods often fine-tune large language models (LLMs) for representation learning, they typically lack robust mechanisms for handling complex and implicit semantics. While Retrieval-GRPO (R-GRPO) recently introduced reinforcement learning to dense retrieval, it suffers from noisy top-K candidates due to limited batch sampling and biased relevance assessments caused by using similarly trained LLMs as reward models. To tackle these issues, we propose Supervised Retrieval-GRPO with Semantic Identifiers (SSR-GRPO). Specifically, our method first proposes a dual-perspective framework for relevance assessment. It leverages both Semantic Identifiers (SIDs) produced by quantization learning and dense representation vectors to generate more unbiased relevance scores. Furthermore, leveraging the hierarchical similarity relationships of the generated SIDs, we mine a set of hard negative samples that serve two purposes: (1) to design a masking function integrated into R-GRPO, effectively filtering intra-group noisy samples; and (2) to construct a Retrieval-DPO task composed of positive and negative sample pairs, enabling the model to capture fine-grained semantic distinctions from a pair-wise perspective. By integrating these optimization strategies, we propose SSR-GRPO. Extensive offline and online experiments validate SSR-GRPO's effectiveness, and it has been deployed on a large-scale e-commerce platform.

</details>


### [180] [Training-Free LLM-Based Recommendation with Post-LLM Item Refinement Using Collaborative Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19665)
*Kyungho Kim,Sunwoo Kim,Geon Lee,Shinhwan Kang,Sojeong Kim,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Donald Loveland,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CoRRe是一个无需训练的推荐框架，采用后LLM范式将协同过滤信号注入LLM生成的物品表示中，通过物品共购图优化嵌入方向和流行度调整幅度，在真实数据集上超越现有无训练方法，性能媲美或优于有训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)在无训练推荐中显示潜力，但LLM生成的用户兴趣往往过于宽泛，难以进行细粒度物品检索。现有方法通过候选重排序或提示增强在LLM前注入CF信号，增益有限。

Method: CoRRe提出后LLM范式，将CF信号注入LLM生成的物品表示，再与LLM生成的用户兴趣匹配排序。具体使用物品-物品共购图优化物品嵌入方向，用物品流行度调整嵌入幅度。

Result: 在真实数据集实验中，CoRRe持续超越现有无训练方法，性能具有竞争力或优于有训练方法，且无需任何模型训练或任务特定微调。

Conclusion: CoRRe通过后LLM注入CF信号有效解决了LLM生成用户兴趣过于宽泛的问题，实现了无需训练即可达到最优推荐性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for training-free recommendation, but LLM-generated user interests are often too broad for fine-grained item retrieval. Existing methods incorporate collaborative filtering (CF) signals in a pre-LLM manner through candidate reranking or prompt augmentation, yielding limited gains. We propose CoRRe, a training-free recommendation framework with a post-LLM paradigm that injects CF signals into LLM-generated item representations, which are later matched with LLM-generated user interests for ranking. Specifically, CoRRe refines the directions of item embeddings using an item-item co-purchase graph and their magnitudes using item popularity. Experiments on real-world datasets show that CoRRe consistently outperforms existing training-free methods and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with training-based methods, without requiring any model training or task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [181] [Do Sequential Recommendation Benchmarks Really Require Higher-Order Sequence Modelling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19833)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Praveen Chandar,Paul N. Bennett,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文通过两个简单的基于近期加权的成对探针（SeqRules和PCTM）评估了广泛使用的序列推荐基准，发现这些探针在多个数据集上优于Transformer模型（eSASRec和SASRec），表明现有基准难以衡量高阶序列建模的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究广泛使用的序列推荐基准是否真正需要复杂的高阶序列建模能力（如Transformer架构），还是简单的近期加权成对模型即可达到甚至超过性能。

Method: 提出两个不学习高阶序列表示的简单探针：Sequential Rules (SeqRules) 和 Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM)。采用eSASRec的评估协议，在多个Amazon数据集、MovieLens-1M和MovieLens-20M上与eSASRec复现版及采样softmax SASRec复现版对比。

Result: 在三个Amazon数据集和MovieLens-1M上，至少一个探针比eSASRec复现版高15-38%和4.4%；但在MovieLens-20M上落后27.3%。在其余四个数据集上，至少一个探针比采样softmax SASRec复现版高9-28%。

Conclusion: 广泛使用的序列推荐基准大多不适合衡量高阶序列建模的增益。将Transformer模型与强近期加权成对探针对比，可作为判断基准是否能有意义衡量高阶建模收益的具体测试。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders increasingly use language-model architectures designed to capture complex, context-dependent interactions. Yet it remains unclear whether widely used benchmarks actually require this modelling capacity. We investigate this question using two simple, recency-weighted pairwise probes that do not learn higher-order sequence representations: Sequential Rules (SeqRules) and our Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM). Using the evaluation protocol of eSASRec, at least one probe exceeds our eSASRec reproduction by 15-38% on three Amazon datasets and by 4.4% on MovieLens-1M, but trails it by 27.3% on MovieLens-20M. On the four remaining datasets, at least one probe also outperforms our sampled-softmax SASRec reproduction by 9-28%, suggesting that these widely used benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring gains from higher-order sequence modelling. More broadly, comparing Transformer-based models against strong recency-weighted pairwise probes provides a concrete test of whether a benchmark can meaningfully measure gains from higher-order sequence modelling.

</details>


### [182] [SCoRD: Semantic-Assisted Continual Retriever-Reranker Distillation for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19998)
*Seunghyun Baek,Gyuseok Lee,Seunghan Lee,Wonbin Kweon,Dong Wang,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出SCoRD，一种用于非平稳数据流下基于LLM的重排序流水线的持续知识蒸馏框架，通过语义推理助手提取意图级指导，实现检索器与重排序器的高效协同适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统采用两阶段流水线（基于ID的检索器+基于LLM的重排序器），需持续适应不断变化的用户兴趣和交互。简单方案是反复更新LLM重排序器并蒸馏知识，但成本极高；仅更新检索器成本低但容量有限，难以从稀疏数据中适应。

Method: SCoRD引入语义推理助手，将LLM推断潜在用户意图的能力蒸馏为可复用的意图级指导；在低置信度序列上选择性蒸馏重排序器知识给检索器；指导仅检索器更新而无需重复LLM推理；将检索器衍生的表示和意图漂移信号反馈给重排序器。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，SCoRD实现了有效且高效的检索器-重排序器协同适应。

Conclusion: SCoRD为基于LLM的重排序流水线在非平稳数据流下提供了一种持续知识蒸馏框架，通过语义推理助手和双向知识传递，解决了成本与适应性的权衡，实现了检索器与重排序器的高效协同进化。

Abstract: Recommendation systems increasingly adopt a two-stage pipeline, where an ID-based retriever retrieves candidates and an LLM-based reranker refines their rankings. To improve retrieval quality, reranker-to-retriever distillation is commonly used to transfer the reranker's knowledge to the retriever. For practical deployment, however, this pipeline must continually adapt to evolving interests and incoming interactions. A naive solution is to repeatedly update the LLM reranker and distill its latest knowledge, but this incurs prohibitive costs. Updating the retriever alone is cheaper, but its limited capacity makes adaptation from sparse data difficult. We propose SCoRD, a continual knowledge distillation framework for LLM-based reranking pipelines under a non-stationary data stream. SCoRD introduces a semantic reasoning assistant that distills the LLM's ability to infer underlying user intents into reusable intent-level guidance. It selectively distills reranker knowledge to the retriever on low-confidence sequences, guides retriever-only updates without repeated LLM inference, and feeds retriever-derived representations and intent-drift signals back to the reranker. Experiments on real-world datasets show that SCoRD enables effective and efficient retriever-reranker co-adaptation.

</details>


### [183] [Daedalus-150M: A Convolution-Attention Hybrid Designed for CPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20210)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种专为CPU推理设计的小语言模型，采用混合架构：6个全注意力块 + 12个短卷积块（仅2个时间步内存），在59.9B tokens上从头训练，以47.31分超越同规模基线（42.20）及多个训练数据量大3-6倍的模型，且在长上下文解码速度上比全注意力模型快1.76倍、比外部同规模模型快2.08倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小模型通常先按大模型方式构建再压缩到CPU，本文反其道而行：首先锁定目标硬件（单用户、逐token、4-bit权重、普通CPU），再设计适配架构。核心动机是解决KV缓存随对话增长导致的内存带宽瓶颈，让三分之二网络永不重读增长的缓存。

Method: 混合架构：18层中仅6层使用全注意力，其余12层采用短卷积（内存宽度固定为2个时间步，与上下文长度无关）。模型从头在59.9B tokens上训练，采用4-bit量化。为隔离架构效应，同规模同数据训练了全注意力基线，并预先设定评测指标。

Result: 主模型在五任务基准得47.31（基线42.20），击败GPT-2 124M、Pythia-160M、OPT-125M、GPT-neo-125M（后者训练数据量大3-6倍）并超越MobileLLM-125M（见过1T tokens）。消融实验显示：混合模型在选定质量指标上胜出0.81%，下游任务持平，4-bit体积减小6.3%，2048上下文解码快1.76倍（对外部同规模模型快2.08倍），速度优势随上下文增长而扩大（带宽计算仅预测1.17倍）。负面发现：4-bit量化未缓解的质量损失、约半数卷积通道失活且无法移除、词表规模超出模型容量承载。

Conclusion: 针对目标硬件定制架构（混合注意力+短卷积）能显著降低KV缓存增长带来的带宽压力，实现长上下文下的超线性加速，证明架构设计比单纯压缩更有效。但4-bit量化损失、冗余卷积通道和过大词表仍待解决。

Abstract: Small language models are usually built like large ones and then squeezed onto a CPU afterwards. We did the opposite: we fixed the target first, one user, one token at a time, 4-bit weights, ordinary CPU, and chose the architecture to suit it. The result keeps full attention in only 6 of its 18 blocks. The other 12 use short convolutions whose memory is two timesteps wide no matter how long the conversation gets, so two thirds of the network never re-reads a growing cache.
  Trained from scratch on 59.9B tokens, the model scores 47.31 on a five-task benchmark against a bar of 42.20 that was fixed before training began. It beats GPT-2 124M, Pythia-160M, OPT-125M and GPT-neo-125M, all trained on three to six times more data, and exceeds MobileLLM-125M's published score despite that model seeing a trillion tokens. Validation bits-per-byte is 0.8685.
  To check the architecture rather than the training recipe, we trained a conventional all-attention model of the same size on the same data, and wrote down the winning condition before scoring either. The hybrid won the chosen quality metric by 0.81%, matched it on downstream tasks, produced a 6.3% smaller 4-bit file, and decoded 1.76x faster at 2048 tokens of context, 2.08x against an external model of similar size. In every measurement the speed advantage is near zero at an empty context and grows with length, which is what the mechanism predicts and what a merely leaner model would not show. A simple bandwidth calculation predicts only 1.17x, so memory volume alone does not explain the gap.
  We also report what did not work: an unmitigated 4-bit quality cost, roughly half the convolution channels ending up inert and impossible to remove, and a vocabulary larger than this model size warrants.

</details>


### [184] [What Makes a Good Fiqh Retriever? Answer Retrieval for Arabic Islamic Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20246)
*Somaya Eltanbouly,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mutaz al-Khatib,Shahd Gaben,Mohammed Ghaly*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了阿拉伯语教法（fiqh）检索测试集，研究“含答案检索”（answer-bearing retrieval），系统评估了密集、词法、混合、微调及教法学派感知等检索策略，发现微调将MRR@5提升至0.553，学派感知过滤在学派特定问题上使MRR@5翻倍以上，主要难点在于区分含裁决段落与主题相似但无裁决段落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伊斯兰问答系统多采用端到端评估，难以将检索失败与生成失败分离；教法问答要求段落必须明确给出问题所需的具体裁决（ruling），而非仅主题相关，因此需要专门的含答案检索基准与细粒度评估。

Method: 1. 构建阿拉伯语教法检索测试集，标注“含答案”相关性（段落需陈述所需裁决）。2. 对比评估密集检索、词法检索、混合检索、微调密集模型及教法学派感知过滤策略。3. 在学派特定问题子集上测试学派感知过滤效果。4. 开展错误分析，定位主要失效模式。

Result: 最佳基线检索器MRR@5为0.524；微调后提升至0.553。混合检索对强模型增益有限；教法学派感知过滤在学派特定问题上使MRR@5提升一倍以上。错误分析显示核心挑战是区分含裁决段落与主题相似但不含裁决段落。

Conclusion: 教法问答的检索需以“含具体裁决”为相关性标准，微调与领域知识（学派感知）能显著提升检索质量；未来工作应聚焦于细粒度裁决匹配与难负例区分，以解决主题相似但无答案段落的干扰。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation is used for Islamic question answering, but most systems are evaluated end-to-end, making retrieval failures difficult to isolate from generation failures. We study answer-bearing retrieval for Arabic fiqh, where a passage is relevant only if it states the ruling required by the question. We build a retrieval test collection for Arabic fiqh and use it to evaluate dense, lexical, hybrid, fine-tuned, and madhhab-aware retrieval strategies. The best retriever achieves 0.524 MRR@5, while fine-tuning improves performance to 0.553. Hybrid retrieval provides limited gains for strong models, whereas madhhab-aware filtering more than doubles MRR@5 on school-specific questions. We further present an error analysis showing that the main challenge is distinguishing answer-bearing passages from topically similar passages that do not contain the requested ruling.

</details>


### [185] [Projecting BrowseComp-Plus onto ClimbMix: Toward More Realistic Corpora for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20317)
*Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Yijun Ge,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 BrowseComp-Plus_CM 基准，将 BrowseComp-Plus 的问题证据迁移至 NVIDIA 发布的 400B-token、5.53亿文档的 ClimbMix 预训练语料库中，并设计了一套投影管道：将问题分解为原子推理步骤，在新语料库中逐步定位证据，经自动验证、独立智能体及人工三重确认后保留问题。管道具有数据集无关性，可推广至任何可分解为可验证事实的基准。实验表明，投影显著增加检索难度：最强智能体答案准确率下降5分，证据召回率从84.3%骤降至21.4%，搜索调用次数增加63%。论文开源了管道、基准及分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BrowseComp-Plus 使用仅含约10万文档的自构建语料库，证据与干扰项均针对特定查询选取，无法公正评估智能体的检索能力。作者希望利用大规模、与基准无关的预训练语料库（ClimbMix）重新定位证据，从而将评估重心从推理转移到检索，并建立一套通用的、可自动化验证的投影流程。

Method: 1. 问题分解：将每个问题拆解为原子推理跳；2. 证据定位：在 ClimbMix 中为每一跳检索支持文档；3. 三重验证：自动验证器、独立智能体、人工评审三方均确认所有跳骤均有证据支持时才保留该问题；4. 相关性标注：输出问题级相关性判断。该管道不依赖特定数据集，适用于任何可分解为可验证事实的基准。

Result: 在 830 道 BrowseComp-Plus 测试题上，管道产出 57 道完全落地的问题。最强智能体答案准确率下降 5 分，证据召回率从 84.3% 跌至 21.4%，搜索调用次数增加 63%，表明难度已成功转移至检索环节。

Conclusion: BrowseComp-Plus_CM 通过将证据迁移至大规模无偏预训练语料库并引入严格投影管道，构建了首个能将智能体检索能力与推理能力解耦的基准。实验证实该基准显著提升检索难度，为评估智能体搜索能力提供了更真实的测试平台。作者已开源管道、基准及分析代码，并计划推出系列投影基准。

Abstract: The BrowseComp-Plus benchmark disentangled the evaluation of agentic search by replacing opaque web search with a fixed corpus, so that an agent's role can be separated from the retriever's. That corpus, however, holds only about 100K documents and was assembled from the supporting documents of the benchmark's own queries plus mined hard negatives, so the evidence and the distractors were both selected per query. We introduce $\text{BrowseComp-Plus}_{\text{CM}}$, which keeps the BrowseComp-Plus questions but relocates their evidence to ClimbMix, a 400B-token, 553M-document mixture of web text released by NVIDIA for pre-training language models and built without reference to any benchmark. Our main contribution is the projection pipeline that makes this possible: it decomposes each question into atomic reasoning hops and grounds every hop in the new corpus, retaining a question only when automatic verification, an independent agent, and human review all confirm that every hop is supported. The pipeline is dataset-agnostic and applies to any benchmark whose questions decompose into verifiable facts. Applied to the 830 BrowseComp-Plus test questions, our pipeline yields 57 fully grounded questions with question-level relevance judgments. Projection shifts the difficulty onto retrieval, as the strongest agent we evaluate loses five points of answer accuracy but sees its evidence recall fall from 84.3% to 21.4% while issuing 63% more search calls. As the first of a series of projections, we release the pipeline, the benchmark, and our analyses at https://github.com/castorini/cmass.

</details>
